PREFACE 


This brochure contains an index to the Gazette of India for 
the month of June, 1956 . Notifications relating to Acts, Ordin 
ances and Regulations (Part II, Section I ); and Statutory 
Rules and Orders (Part II , Sections 3 and 4 ) have been indexed 
exhaustively . As regards other Notifications (Part I , Sections I 
and 3 , Part III , Section 1 ) only items of importance from Mem 
bers view point have been indexed . References to the Parts and 
Sections of Gazette along with the page numbers have been given 
in the index for facility of reference . 


NEW DELHI; 
The IIth December , 1956 . 


M . N . KAUL , 

Secretary
